([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rare-cell-cluster-separation-assessment))
- **rare-cell-clustering-method-benchmarking**: Use when a new rare-cell or subcluster clustering method must be validated for label-recall ity on simulated scRNA-seq data with known ground-truth cell-type labels, comparing ARI/NMI/silhouette against clustering baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rare-cell-clustering-method-benchmarking))
- **rare-cell-clustering-with-dropclust**: Use when you already have per-cell rareness scores (FiRE) and IQR-thresholded rare cells (e.g. 727 cells from ~20k Arc-ME profiles) and need to partition them into minor sub-clusters for marker-based annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rare-cell-clustering-with-dropclust))
- **rare-cell-count-validation**: Use when reproducing FiRE rare-cell calls on ~68k-cell scRNA-seq data and you must verify both the expected rare-cell count (4238) and cluster-level specificity of top-scoring cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rare-cell-count-validation))
- **rare-cell-detection-benchmarking**: Use when comparing rare-cell-type identification methods on scRNA-seq data, or when quantifying how few differentially expressed genes a detector needs and how runtimes scale to tens of thousands of profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rare-cell-detection-benchmarking))
- **rare-cell-detection-method-benchmarking**: Use when you need to quantify and compare rare cell detection accuracy of scCAD against FiRE, GapClust, and GiniClust3, e.g. reproducing the DE-gene robustness/sensitivity analysis (Supplementary Fig. 11) on artificial or 1%Jurkat.h5 data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rare-cell-detection-method-benchmarking))
- **rare-cell-gap-distance-quantification**: Use when you have a large scRNA-seq expression matrix and need per-cell nearest-neighbour distance statistics (D&lt;m,k&gt;, ∆D&lt;m,k&gt;, ∆∆D&lt;m,k&gt;, skewness&lt;k&gt;) to detect rare cell clusters via inter-cluster gaps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rare-cell-gap-distance-quantification))
- **rare-cell-identification**: Use when you need per-cell rareness scores from a large scRNA-seq expression matrix (tens of thousands of cells) and clustering-based rare cell tools (RaceID, GiniClust) are too slow or memory inefficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rare-cell-identification))
- **rare-cell-population-denoising-assessment**: Use when comparing denoising performance of single-cell methods on downsampled count data, especially when rare cell states (e.g., pericytes, microfold cells, microglial cells) are present and neighbor-smoothing may bias results. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rare-cell-population-denoising-assessment))
- **rare-cell-population-detection**: Use when a single-cell RNA-seq dataset is too large for downstream analysis (e.g., &gt;200k cells) and a rare cell population must be recovered without uniform subsampling that overlooks rare states. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rare-cell-population-detection))
- **rare-cell-population-identification**: Use when a broadly-defined scRNA-seq cluster is suspected heterogeneous (e.g. a stromal/mesenchymal cluster that may hide CAF subtypes, or immune clusters hiding fine-grained subtypes) and rare, low-abundance subpopulations must be recovered from Seurat-processed data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rare-cell-population-identification))
- **rare-cell-subtype-frequency-quantification**: Use when clustering an already-annotated single-cell subset (e.g. SingleR-annotated T/NK cells from RA patient PBMCs) and the goal is to resolve rare subtypes and doublets and report their cluster frequencies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rare-cell-subtype-frequency-quantification))
- **rare-cell-type-cluster-purity-analysis**: Use when evaluating whether an iterative cluster decomposition/merging step improves the purity (occupy rate) of rare cell types in their dominant clusters, as in scCAD's I-cluster vs. M-cluster comparison (Supplementary Table 9). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rare-cell-type-cluster-purity-analysis))
- **rare-cell-type-embedding-separation**: Use when scRNA-seq data contain dominant/common cell types plus rare populations and the goal is a 2D embedding that visibly separates the rare clusters, evaluated via silhouette index against t-SNE and UMAP baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rare-cell-type-embedding-separation))
- **rare-cell-type-multistage-clustering**: Use when a single-cell RNA-seq clustering run merges a rare annotated cell type into a larger cluster and a multi-stage re-clustering could isolate it. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rare-cell-type-multistage-clustering))
- **rare-cell-type-recovery-quantification**: Use when testing whether oversampling rare cell types (e.g. CD4T, mast, plasma, pDC, cholangiocyte) improves reference-based single-cell annotation accuracy, and you need a per-type F1/count comparison table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rare-cell-type-recovery-quantification))
- **rare-cell-type-representation-assessment**: Use when a subsampled single-cell dataset (e.g. a scValue, GeoSketch, Sphetcher, TreeHopper, KH, or Uniform sketch) must be checked for loss of rare cell types before downstream ML/DL tasks such as cell-type annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rare-cell-type-representation-assessment))
- **rare-celltype-balancing-for-classification**: Use when preparing a cell-by-gene reference matrix (e.g., SELINA xx_expr.txt with paired xx_meta.txt labels) for supervised cell-type annotation and some cell types are far outnumbered by majority types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rare-celltype-balancing-for-classification))
